FULL CURRENT REQUIREMENTS:
1 Reusable app-agnostic Android MCP bridge. ALL available SDK/Binder/AIDL/protocol inventory, generic/raw invocation, source/version evidence, unknown/unreachable/private/OEM reasons and explicit permission boundaries. No curated omissions or universal private/OEM introspection. Review the mechanism, not actual Google catalog coverage. Business decisions/monitoring schedulers belong to consuming agents.
2 All-Kotlin server plus Kotlin Jetpack Compose management UI supersedes Avalonia/.NET UI. One coherent Android packaging/process and bounded unexported local service facade. Kotlin service owns transport, policy, SQLite, resolver, invocation, callbacks/groups/lifecycle; UI cannot bypass protected owner authorization. Persistent service independent of UI, optional boot with honest Android limits and running status AppBar/notification, separate MCP/ADB/device-view health. Client mapping, permissions and configurable logging in SQLite. No actual implementation/deployment requested in this phase.
3 Same-device paired ADB for uninstrumented targets; remote-host loopback forwarding. screen.state/input.tap are on-demand. Generic phone control must not require embedding a runtime in target apps.
4 Internal Kotlin port of pinned SharpNinja.Avalonia.RemoteControl preserving ARC protocol. Distinct controller UI/ARC client and target listener/router/auth/adapters, Compose tree/input/frame adapter, separate ADB backend. Raw TCP signed4-byte BE length max8388608, protocol envelope1.0 vs package0.8.0, protobuf exact tags/enums/defaults/unknown handling; one request/socket, repeated stream responses and terminal end flag; no separate handshake; Cancel8 declared but unsupported, real cancellation closes socket. Bearer field4, no fake TLS/IsAdbTunnel, OK envelope can contain command failure, upstream FPS parsed not enforced. Mouse/wheel/key/text, no multitouch; Avalonia names not Android keycodes. Stable/stale node generation, coordinate/density semantics, ordered input, protected approval/secret surfaces. Golden cross-language fixtures/test plan and license-notice distribution gate, without claiming port implemented.
5 Versioned provenance catalog/permissions/CRUD with immutable imports, local overrides, strong ETag/CAS/history/transitions. Declared permission metadata distinct from runtime OS/backend authority and MCP per-client grant; generated/generic/raw paths converge to canonical policy. No local UI/API/ARC self-approval bypass.
6 Startup compatible bindings AND types from observed Android major/minor, SDK extensions, build/OEM, installed module/interface versions+hashes and supported reachability. Unknown/unavailable distinction, full inventory retained, coherent type closure and atomic activation, cache invalidation/refresh on device/backend/catalog changes. No API-level-only or source-version-as-installed guesses. Binder engine acquisition/codecs require verified exact wire contract and explicit unsupported reasons; no magical universal access.
7 ALL callback roles/candidates including SDK listeners/delegates/abstract classes/inherited methods and Binder endpoints; source/version evidence, exact pairing/argument/token positions, unknown classification. Generic invoke callback setup returns finite subscription handle. Standard MCP resource journal baseline, explicit negotiated optional direct stream, event identity/correlation/sequence/time/payload/errors, ordering/delivery/replay/dedup, bounded buffers/overflow/backpressure/oversize, cancellation/unregister/death/revocation/disconnect/restart and per-client auth/logging. Synchronous return callbacks must use bounded LOCAL typed response/deadline/fallback or unsupported, never remote-agent latency.
8 Small coherent functional groups and job-description discovery, not packages or giant tool lists. Full searchable unassigned inventory. Bounded ranked summaries/pagination/token budgets and dependencies/applicability/grant status; deterministic index, optional semantic ranking not authority. Session/client activation/deactivation, stable IDs/hashes, atomic paired registration/cleanup/type closure, CAS/epochs/cache invalidation/isolation/inflight behavior. Discovery/activation cannot grant permission. Portable bootstrap/manifest/genericinvoke fallback; selective dynamic alias loading is opt-in, not guaranteed for all hosts.
9 Pinned MCP2025-11-25: POST SSE eventually one final JSONRPC response then normally terminates, not infinite tools/call or extra finals. GET SSE/stdio can carry standard notifications. resources/subscribe exact URI, resources/updated URI only; resources/read finite bounded journal pages/status. Transport Last-Event-ID distinct from application cursor. Progress ends with request. Custom direct payload transport explicitly negotiated/authenticated with fallback; never invented standard methods. tools/list pagination/list_changed cannot guarantee lazy host context or eviction. Origin/auth/session/reconnect precise.

Structurizr lacks native code/class views: documented local image-view + inline PlantUML class/code workflow, explicitly labeled, matching C4 components. Detailed server and ARC/UI classes/interfaces ownership/multiplicity/responsibility. Not fake UML component boxes.

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT {"file": "arc-upstream/19792a52a13a3df342f90bab6b246b547e673919/src/Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol/BridgeFrameCodec.cs", "size_bytes": 5485, "sha256": "fc0f60ddcdbab4c51804f6620f064064c0b7acf6331507c8207e6be390476e99"}
using System.Buffers.Binary;
using Google.Protobuf;

namespace Avalonia.RemoteControl.Protocol;

/// <summary>
/// Encodes and decodes length-prefixed protobuf bridge frames.
/// </summary>
public static class BridgeFrameCodec
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Number of bytes in the big-endian frame length prefix.
    /// </summary>
    public const int LengthPrefixByteCount = 4;

    /// <summary>
    /// Default maximum bridge frame payload size.
    /// </summary>
    public const int DefaultMaxFrameLength = 8 * 1024 * 1024;

    /// <summary>
    /// Encodes a protobuf message as a length-prefixed bridge frame.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="message">Message to encode.</param>
    /// <returns>Length-prefixed frame bytes.</returns>
    public static byte[] Encode(IMessage message)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(message);

        var payload = message.ToByteArray();
        var frame = new byte[LengthPrefixByteCount + payload.Length];
        BinaryPrimitives.WriteInt32BigEndian(frame.AsSpan(0, LengthPrefixByteCount), payload.Length);
        payload.CopyTo(frame.AsSpan(LengthPrefixByteCount));
        return frame;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Decodes a complete length-prefixed bridge frame.
    /// </summary>
    /// <typeparam name="T">Message type.</typeparam>
    /// <param name="frame">Complete frame bytes.</param>
    /// <param name="parser">Message parser.</param>
    /// <param name="maxFrameLength">Maximum accepted payload size.</param>
    /// <returns>Decoded protobuf message.</returns>
    public static T Decode<T>(
        ReadOnlySpan<byte> frame,
        MessageParser<T> parser,
        int maxFrameLength = DefaultMaxFrameLength)
        where T : IMessage<T>
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(parser);

        if (frame.Length < LengthPrefixByteCount)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException("Bridge frame did not contain a complete length prefix.");
        }

        var length = BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32BigEndian(frame[..LengthPrefixByteCount]);
        ValidateLength(length, maxFrameLength);

        if (frame.Length - LengthPrefixByteCount != length)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException("Bridge frame length did not match the payload length.");
        }

        return parser.ParseFrom(frame[LengthPrefixByteCount..]);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Writes a protobuf message as a length-prefixed bridge frame.
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="stream">Target stream.</param>
    /// <param name="message">Message to write.</param>
    /// <param name="cancellationToken">Cancellation token.</param>
    /// <returns>A task representing the write.</returns>
    public static async ValueTask WriteAsync(
        Stream stream,
        IMessage message,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(stream);
        var frame = Encode(message);
        await stream.WriteAsync(frame, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Reads one length-prefixed protobuf bridge frame from a stream.
    /// </summary>
    /// <typeparam name="T">Message type.</typeparam>
    /// <param name="stream">Source stream.</param>
    /// <param name="parser">Message parser.</param>
    /// <param name="maxFrameLength">Maximum accepted payload size.</param>
    /// <param name="cancellationToken">Cancellation token.</param>
    /// <returns>Decoded protobuf message.</returns>
    public static async ValueTask<T> ReadAsync<T>(
        Stream stream,
        MessageParser<T> parser,
        int maxFrameLength = DefaultMaxFrameLength,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        where T : IMessage<T>
    {
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(stream);
        ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(parser);

        var lengthBuffer = new byte[LengthPrefixByteCount];
        await ReadExactlyAsync(stream, lengthBuffer, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
        var length = BinaryPrimitives.ReadInt32BigEndian(lengthBuffer);
        ValidateLength(length, maxFrameLength);

        var payload = new byte[length];
        await ReadExactlyAsync(stream, payload, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
        return parser.ParseFrom(payload);
    }

    private static async ValueTask ReadExactlyAsync(
        Stream stream,
        byte[] buffer,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var offset = 0;
        while (offset < buffer.Length)
        {
            var read = await stream.ReadAsync(
                buffer.AsMemory(offset, buffer.Length - offset),
                cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);
            if (read == 0)
            {
                throw new EndOfStreamException("Bridge stream ended before a complete frame was read.");
            }

            offset += read;
        }
    }

    private static void ValidateLength(int length, int maxFrameLength)
    {
        if (maxFrameLength < 1)
        {
            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(maxFrameLength), "Maximum frame length must be positive.");
        }

        if (length < 0)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException("Bridge frame length was negative.");
        }

        if (length > maxFrameLength)
        {
            throw new InvalidDataException(
                $"Bridge frame length {length} exceeds the maximum allowed length {maxFrameLength}.");
        }
    }
}

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT {"file": "catalog/SOURCE-METADATA-CONTRACT.md", "size_bytes": 12132, "sha256": "42d41a92934fe68d608175a9f231a89b25942e1154f42a60a117efc600f3d29e"}
# Source metadata integration contract 1.0.0

This document specifies offline catalog production and a SQLite reference oracle.
It does not implement a Kotlin service, HTTP listener, callback runtime, group
activation, device probe or verified wire codec. Current design remains separate.

## Canonical representation

`Entry.extensions.source_contract_metadata` is a `SourceContractMetadataSet`:
`schema_version`, `entry_id`, and nonempty `variants`. Each variant is a
`SourceContractMetadata` envelope tied to one immutable declaration occurrence.
The optional addition keeps the Entry 1.1 envelope; the embedded contract has its
own 1.0.0 version and strict known fields. Unknown later contract versions must
remain preserved as opaque import evidence, explicitly unsupported for runtime
interpretation; do not silently normalize them into this version.

An exact variant carries source IDs and the SHA-256 of its original
`binding_declarations.document_json` bytes. `attach_occurrences` verifies those
bytes, identity and provenance before generating facts. No one-way flags, inherited
type shapes or argument roles are borrowed from the aggregate effective Entry.
Without exact occurrence context, `aggregate-unresolved` is retained with a
reason, unknown reply mode and no inferred inheritance. Duplicate exact contexts
are rejected. The helper is a build hook before effective Entry serialization,
record hashing and export; it does not mutate original declaration rows.

Source IDs depend on processing provenance. Join evidence using verified
`(repository, revision, path, sha256)` against the current build's source registry,
then bind its new source ID. Never carry release011 source IDs into a new build.
Do not match on a basename, owner name, descriptor literal or API level alone.
The importer must reject a join whose byte hash, repository or revision differs.
`evidence_lookup` is trusted importer code, not a request-supplied callback.

## Evidence and uncertainty

`interface_evidence` preserves source-interface-evidence/1 DTOs, ordered published
hash candidates and Soong/APEX availability selectors. A build module is not an
installed Android module identity. Published source candidates do not prove a
runtime interface hash, generated ABI equivalence or installed version range.
`hidl_source_constraints` preserves exact source-hash predicates OR explicit
unknown reasons. Translate a validated predicate into a DeviceResolution Rule
only through its defined clause; do not put the whole proof envelope into a Rule.
No platform API range is inferred from HIDL/AIDL version numbers.

`callback_facts` preserves type disposition, endpoint method, parameter and
lifecycle-pair candidates. AIDL reply mode is one-way if either explicit or
interface-inherited flag is true, synchronous only if both are explicitly false,
and otherwise unknown. HIDL uses its own explicit one-way flag. Other source
languages remain unknown here: Java `void` is not Binder one-way proof. Null
parameter direction stays null. Name heuristics never prove callback direction,
registration, cleanup, threading, return policy or stream semantics.

`functional_facts` preserves annotation facts and namespace search labels. Every
unclassified entry stays searchable; package names do not automatically become
functional groups. Source facts do not populate proven group memberships.

Native evidence includes ABI records, resolved/unresolved source-local type
references, function-pointer parameter candidates and five supported descriptor
declaration families. Preserve raw/computed/unclosed token forms, unresolved build
conditions and unknown runtime registration. ABI local keys/evidence IDs are not
MCP binding IDs. The evidence wrapper fixes confidence to `structural-only` and
runtime observation, wire proof and authority to false. Global native coverage is
not proven by these supported forms. Unmatched evidence remains in a schema-valid
sidecar indexed by source identity, not discarded for lack of an Entry join.

## Declarative resources and owner CRUD

`CallbackDescriptor` and `FunctionalGroup` are separate versioned collections,
not new Entry kinds and not inferred runtime grants. Their exact source contract
hashes pin catalog entities; final device-resolved transitive schema hashes are
computed later. Candidate/unknown callback roles require a reason; proven roles
and cleanup require evidence. Abstract classes require a reviewed, versioned,
hashed prebuilt adapter or explicit unsupported status. No runtime subclassing
or registration follows from saving a descriptor.

Materialization is none for a proven non-callback, unknown with a reason for an
unknown role, and explicitly unsupported when a class needs an absent reviewed
adapter. Concrete non-final class subclassing is distinct from abstract classes.
A candidate's known shape is not proof of callback role or registration.

Groups contain at most 128 explicit members, required types, pinned dependencies
and atomic registration/cleanup units. Unassigned catalog entries remain available
to discovery. All referenced catalog IDs and hashes must be checked against a
trusted current catalog index. Missing index/reference, wrong hash, non-type type
reference, unknown dependency or cyclic group graph is 422. Dependency revisions
remain pinned; changing a dependency invalidates activation of dependents until a
new coherent revision is selected. Administrative `active` does not mean invocable.

The offline `MetadataCatalog` implements immutable base imports and revision
history, local overlays, imported suppression, local tombstones and explicit
conflict rebasing. Owner/importer capabilities are trusted fixture inputs, never
JSON request fields. A future host must enforce protected owner authorization
and read visibility before invoking it; it is not an authentication provider.

Create uses PUT at the ITEM URI with `If-None-Match: *`: success201, existing
identity412. No conditional POST targets an already-existing collection.
Replace PUT uses `If-Match`; exactly one create/replace precondition is allowed.
DELETE requires an exact strong ETag. Missing precondition is 428, malformed is 400,
stale is 412, unauthorized owner is 403, missing identity is 404, invalid document
or reference is 422. PUT replaces the whole declarative document, never PATCH.
DELETE preserves history and returns the suppressed/deleted representation. A
repeat DELETE with the current ETag is idempotent. PUT of a tombstone restores
it. A conflicting newer import requires explicit resolution against its current
base hash; merely sending a fresh ETag does not resolve an overlay conflict.
Retrying a stale conditional DELETE can return412 without further effect; HTTP
idempotence concerns intended effects, not identical responses. GET current state
before a fresh intent. Conflict resolution is mutation-body
`resolution:{action:"rebase"|"retain-local",expected_base_hash:hash|null}`;
the action must match imported-base versus local-only conflict semantics.

`"meta1:<generation>:<kind>:<sha256(resource_id UTF8)>:<base_hash|local>:<revision>:<state>:<content_hash>"`
is the strong metadata ETag. A successful head change increments the global
metadata generation atomically, invalidating all old metadata cursors and ETags.
This is conservative metadata-view CAS: unrelated writes can cause a false
conflict. The public representation contains its current ETag and therefore also
changes. No per-resource edit independence or high-write-rate pagination is
claimed. Clients reload/rebase exact intent; bounded listing retries eventually
report busy/restart instead of retrying indefinitely. The closed ETag state set
is active/suppressed/deleted/conflict.

Content encoding is `meta-json-v1`, mapped to document schema_version1.0.0:
CPython3.14 JSON encoder semantics with `sort_keys=True`, `separators=(',',':')`,
`ensure_ascii=False`, `allow_nan=False`, then strict UTF-8. Keys sort by Unicode
scalar/code-point order, not UTF-16 code units; strings keep their code points
without normalization. Quote/backslash and control characters use the encoder's
standard JSON escapes; slash and valid non-ASCII are unescaped. Integers render
exact decimal; finite binary64 floats use its shortest-roundtrip repr, including
integral-float decimal points, negative zero and exponent spelling: `1` differs
from `1.0`; examples are `-0.0`, `1e-07`, `1e+20`. Non-finite numbers, lone
surrogates and non-JSON keys are rejected. The offline digest and literal-byte
golden vectors are normative; a Kotlin encoder must pass integer/float/Unicode/
escaping fixtures before interoperability. This is not a JCS claim. Original
source/declaration hashes verify original bytes without re-serialization. A
canonicalization change requires a versioned migration.
Base/import hashes and local history cannot be edited or
deleted. SQL null cannot satisfy schema-version or identity checks. SQLite FK,
JSON checks and immutability triggers supplement full JSON Schema/semantic
validation; SQL alone does not prove every descriptor field or reference.

Read/list/history HTTP routes are specifications in OpenAPI, not a deployed API.
Metadata list cursors must pin authenticated reader, collection, registry and
metadata generation; bounded keyset pagination, maximum 100 resources, rejects
cross-reader/stale cursors with 410. Retrying the same cursor under unchanged pins
is allowed for a lost response; it is not consume-once. Non-active/history visibility is
owner-only. Source metadata reads follow Entry read visibility; absence is an
explicit `not-produced` result with reason, never an empty proof of no callbacks.
This offline oracle currently exercises generation/reader cursor invalidation;
HTTP serialization, opaque authenticated cursors and middleware remain host work.
The trusted index is the currently published sealed catalog, never a staged
import. Later suppression or contract/type changes leave historical resources
inspectable but make activation fail stale/unavailable until an explicit coherent
revision is validated. A stored descriptor or group never grants authority.

HTTP semantics follow [RFC9110](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9110.html): opaque
strong ETags, item-target creation preconditions and idempotent intended effects.
The unshipped OpenAPI1.5 collection-create draft is superseded by1.6; source DTO
schema1.0 stays separate. No HTTP host is implemented by the reference.

## Integration and validation

1. Merge the exact canonical schemas/helpers into an isolated source candidate.
   Preserve the source owner's newer parser changes. Copy helper hashes exactly.
2. Add the occurrence hook before `content=js(doc); digest=sha(content)`. Query
   original declaration JSON plus its stored hash; do not use projected or
   aggregate JSON as the immutable hash input. A trusted occurrence projection
   may supply additional source-specific facts only with separately bound proof.
3. Retain source sidecars for unjoined native/interface evidence. Unknown records
   must remain visible, including 173 unresolved HIDL constraints in the tested
   export. Do not fabricate runtime Rule predicates from source selectors.
4. Validate Entry and metadata, re-hash the effective document and regenerate all
   catalogs/SQLite/export manifests together. Keep old published releases immutable.
5. Run normal and optimized suites, real-export structural validation and full
   publication/schema/FK/hash reconciliation on the rebuilt candidate. Exact
   producer/contract hashes must accompany the next review snapshot.

Reference fixtures cover the actual builder occurrence -> metadata -> Entry
schema -> new effective hash path, malformed immutable hash rejection,
unknown preservation, identity checks, source/authority separation, CRUD/CAS,
immutable imports/history, rollback, reference pins and dependency/lifecycle
constraints. They do not demonstrate Kotlin runtime callbacks or production
semantic completeness. Combined review017 intentionally uses the earlier frozen
baseline; these later changes require a fresh review before acceptance.

END ARTIFACT catalog/SOURCE-METADATA-CONTRACT.md

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT {"file": "design-c4/README.md", "size_bytes": 4354, "sha256": "25603ebc7d6d7d4187837873f680625e2bcedf2470b3a57f3830f20a0e20f692"}
# Android MCP design documentation

Canonical design: [Markdown](<docs/Android MCP Bridge Design.md>). [Screen definitions](docs/ui-screens-and-storyboards.md). [Storyboard deck](storyboards/storyboard.md). Design and offline tooling only: no deployed Kotlin Android app, phone actions or public service.

Use Gradle8.14.3 with Java21, the exact StructurizrCLI2025.11.09 image digest in toolchain-lock.json, PlantUML1.2025.10 and MarpCLI4.2.3. Existing local image supplies Java/Graphviz. Node26.8.2 was used for local Marp HTML. Dependencies reside under .tools and .gradle-home; no host installation is needed. The Gradle wrapper pins distribution SHA256. Keep these folders out of the portable archive; bootstrap verified dependencies into task-local caches first. The wrapper does not make a fresh cache magically offline-ready.

## Targets

Run ./gradlew --offline validateArchitecture generateArchitectureDiagrams generateUiAssets renderStoryboards verifyDesignDocs packageDesignDocs. The same invocation is used inside the pinned container with only this docs project mounted, NODE_BIN selecting a compatible existing read-only Node runtime, network disabled and no exposed ports. STRUCTURIZR_CLI defaults to the existing image's /usr/local/structurizr-cli/structurizr.sh. The README records the actual invocation and receipts once complete.

validateArchitecture depends on locally rendered code-view images and fails on invalid DSL. generateArchitectureDiagrams exports native C4 views through Structurizr then renders SVGs locally; image views scope the editable inline PlantUML class detail. generateUiAssets copies editable vector screen/storyboard source. renderStoryboards embeds SVG assets into a finite local Marp HTML deck. verifyDesignDocs fails on missing outputs, broken Markdown links or raster/script substitutions. packageDesignDocs creates a deterministic archive with only current docs/SVG/source/build configuration and validation receipts. No diagram is sent to a public renderer and no Structurizr workspace is uploaded.

## Tooling and license

The internal ARC port's pinned source and exact field/enum contract are documented in the design. MIT package metadata was verified upstream; authoritative notices remain a pre-distribution gate for copied/translated schema/code. This package contains the derived design contract, not the upstream source archive or a Kotlin port. Gradle/Structurizr/PlantUML/Marp are build dependencies with their own licenses; the archive includes configuration and wrapper provenance, not downloaded distributions or node_modules.

## Bootstrap and actual isolated build

On a machine with Python3/npm and the pinned existing renderer image available, run python3 tooling/bootstrap.py. It downloads only checksum-recorded Gradle/PlantUML task dependencies and performs npm ci with the included Marp integrity lock, without lifecycle scripts or global installation. Java21/Graphviz come from the documented image; this is separate from any Android app installation. Offline use requires a populated wrapper distribution and Marp cache.

The validated environment uses the official Gradle wrapper in the existing digest-pinned Structurizr container, user1000, network none, no exposed ports, task directory mounted at /workspace and an existing compatible Node binary at /tool-node/node. Node26.8.2 requires libatomic.so.1; an existing host copy is mounted read-only in that ephemeral container, its hash retained in toolchain-lock.json. This is an environment dependency, not a hidden package installation. The strict renderer-lock.json profile checks the exact JDK21.0.8, Node26.8.2, renderer jar hashes and committed/cached Marp lock. An environment mismatch fails rather than claiming byte reproducibility. Alternate runtimes require an explicitly reviewed new lock/profile. No task starts a public diagram server.

Execution and model/docs validation receipts are in validation/ in the archive. The portable ZIP omits .tools, .gradle-home, all source catalog data, reviewer receipts, old diagram outputs, Word/PDF and raster mockups.

Canonical UI source is ui-svg/*.svg; generateUiAssets verifies every manifest ID and copies identical bytes to docs/screens. Current package has35 unique docs SVGs:13 C4 views,5 class views,11 screens and6 storyboards. No Android application is generated.

END ARTIFACT design-c4/README.md

# C4 code-level detail, using UML class notation

Structurizr has no native class view. These editable inline PlantUML definitions are rendered locally and referenced by image views in workspace.dsl. They are proposed Kotlin classes/interfaces, not implemented Android code. Composition diamonds denote owned lifetime; dashed arrows denote dependency, and quoted labels denote multiplicity. Each block identifies its scoped C4 component. Member lists show selected responsibilities, not every Kotlin member. A reference stub explicitly names its authoritative view; realizations inherit the full interface contract. Local epochs/generations are bridge metadata unless the pinned proto has the corresponding field.

## server-core-classes — bridge.app.transport and server ownership

```plantuml
@startuml
```

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT {"file": "design-c4/architecture/round013-design-corrections.md", "size_bytes": 19523, "sha256": "a63b0f88769366b6f9a2a0d0dac66dc506bddc1b669e352c58d5eb1303f15b38"}
> Historical round013 correction record, superseded by the current canonical design. Not a current normative specification; retained for review provenance only.

# Verified design mechanisms after round013

These mechanisms are normative for this revision and replace conflicting earlier wording. They are design contracts, not Kotlin implementation claims. The finding ledger retains all28 original findings, including the brightness claim refuted by the current official source.

## Callback object construction and reachability

CallbackDescriptor.materialization_strategy has closed values interface_dynamic_proxy, binder_generic_stub, prebuilt_adapter and unsupported_abstract_class. A public Java interface can use java.lang.reflect.Proxy with the exact source-proven interface list, declared methods and supported classloader. An AIDL callback uses a reviewed generic android.os.Binder subclass: attach/query the pinned descriptor, implement onTransact using the exact verified transaction IDs, flags and codecs, enforce interface tokens and sender UID/capability before handing off to the broker. Parcel/file-descriptor/Binder-handle lifetimes are bounded and released on every failure. This is not a universal AIDL compiler, non-SDK access bypass or arbitrary Binder-handle import.

An abstract callback class cannot use java.lang.reflect.Proxy. It is registration-unsupported with ABSTRACT_CLASS_NO_RUNTIME_SUBCLASS unless a reviewed prebuilt Kotlin/Java subclass adapter is shipped and its adapter ID/version/hash and supported callback methods are pinned. No runtime dex/bytecode generation or downloaded executable code is selected. The catalog still identifies every abstract callback and its unknown/unreachable variants. SDK/Java invocation resolves public members at admission through java.lang.reflect or supported MethodHandles from the exact pinned JVM signature; do not ship hundreds of thousands of per-member wrappers. R8 keep rules preserve only reviewed bridge adapters and reflective entry points. Non-SDK/access failures are explicit NON_SDK_BLOCKED/OS_UNAVAILABLE reasons, never silently retried through a stronger identity.

Permitted Binder-handle acquisition is (a) public bindService to an explicit exported component with package/signing identity verification, or (b) a handle returned by a documented public SDK handle-source method. Platform ServiceManager lookup and manager-private proxy fields are non-SDK; the ordinary app backend reports backend_unreachable:non_sdk instead of promising access. A separate ADB raw adapter may expose only reviewed fixed service-call operations supported by shell authority and its actual marshalling limits. That is not an app-UID IBinder handle, a generic verified Parcelable codec or permission to use hwbinder. Raw connection_id is either a real connection_profiles.profile_id or reserved platform:local; object handles pin that profile, user, target and backend epoch and cannot migrate between them. HIDL declarations and completion semantics remain inventory/classification. No ordinary app backend is assumed to access hwbinder; unsupported paths report backend_unreachable:hidl. A future separately reviewed privileged backend would require an explicit scope/design change, not an inferred owner grant.

## Startup reuse and bounded admission

A new boot invalidates old admission capabilities, process-local object handles and approvals. It does not require discarding a validated semantic selection cache. Reuse is allowed only if a fresh supported capability observation matches the cache's canonical semantic facts (platform/minor/extensions/build/OEM/module/interface identity and relevant availability), scope, inventory root, catalog/overlay generations, resolver/schema version and type-graph digest. Boot ID, observation time and transient handle IDs are excluded from that semantic key, but remain new runtime evidence. Verify the app-private cache seal against the Keystore-authenticated manifest, open the immutable generation and create a new view revision bound to the new boot/capability epoch; do not mutate/relabel the old view. File identity/generation and on-read page/document hashes detect stale/corrupt data; failure quarantines the cache and closes device admission. Root/full process compromise remains outside the stated trust guarantee. Every runtime permission/reachability/wire/handle check is fresh at dispatch regardless of cache reuse.

For the API level35 (Android15) or later, 8GiB/128GB reference profile and approximately800k-entry inventory, proposed unmeasured p95 acceptance targets are <=600seconds for cold complete indexed resolution and <=10seconds for warm validated semantic reuse. Resolve in bounded batches, SQLite staging and indexed reverse-type worklists: application scratch<=64MiB, SQLite page cache<=64MiB, total service peak<=384MiB and disk scratch<=incoming catalog size. A large dependency closure that exceeds a bound spills to validated SQLite worklists or fails explicitly; it is not silently cut. Recompute affected closures on capability change, and perform full cold rebuild when compatibility keys or dependency provenance are insufficient. While Resolving, only authenticated policy/status, callback.status/callback.cancel and bindings.search/list/get are admitted; discovery reports resolving applicability. Device-affecting calls return VIEW_RESOLVING. Progress and cancellation remain bounded; the listener may be ready while device admission is unavailable, with separate status fields. These targets need real Android benchmarks; the historical Python4.30GiB/565.6second probe does not satisfy them.

## Catalog delivery and management API ownership

Large catalog delivery uses an owner-selected Storage Access Framework content URI, not the256KiB JSON request body. BridgeService obtains the owner-granted read capability, preflights capacity, streams bounded copy chunks into a new app-private staging file, hashes the exact copied bytes and fsyncs before matching the independently trusted manifest size/SHA. Only then open the SQLite input read-only for schema/integrity/FK/content validation and atomic activation. Untrusted filenames, archive paths or SQL are never commands. A bounded expiring staged-local-handle identifies this verified copy; POST /v1/catalog/imports carries that handle plus expected pins/idempotency key. An ADB-pushed file is usable only when the owner exposes it through this same supported URI flow; the app does not assume access to /data/local/tmp or another UID's private directory. Abort/quarantine incomplete or mismatched copies and never activate them.

BridgeService owns the optional /v1/catalog administration route on the same authenticated loopback listener as /mcp, with separate route authorization. It is disabled by default and enabled only by fresh local owner action for a bounded maximum10-minute window. Owner-issued credentials.kind=admin are separate from kind=mcp; admin credentials are rejected by tools/call and ordinary MCP credentials are rejected by admin routes. Admin credential expiry cannot exceed the current management window, revocation closes active admin work before the commit boundary, and every import/mutation needs its exact owner authorization and CAS rules. The local Compose facade invokes the same application service without opening HTTP or minting an admin credential. No admin route is active merely because this design contains its specification. PUT/DELETE are the mutation family; no PATCH alias is defined.

## Callback/group table and operation contracts

These tables are additions to the service configuration model. Immutable version rows have append-only history and evidence; local override revisions remain separate from imported documents. Mandatory FK checking, unique indexes, scalar/check constraints and transactional publication follow the existing SQLite contract. JSON documents are schema-validated before commit; a CHECK on JSON text alone is insufficient. The executable migration/reference integration remains a phase deliverable to validate separately.

| Table / key | Required constraints and indexes |
| --- | --- |
| callback_descriptors PK(callback_id, revision) | revision>=1; type_id and schema_hash pinned to immutable catalog revision; role CHECK proven_callback/candidate/proven_non_callback/unknown; materialization_strategy CHECK the four closed values above; evidence and unknown_reason; immutable document hash. Unknown/candidate cannot become registrable merely by a name heuristic. |
| callback_methods PK(callback_id, revision, method_id) | FK descriptor revision RESTRICT; method/schema/type closure pins; direction and async/sync/completion classification; optional prebuilt adapter and local reply-policy evidence. |
| callback_bindings PK(registration_binding_id, callback_id, callback_revision, formal_position) | position>=0; FK descriptor; exact registration and cleanup IDs/schema pins; token/receiver/argument mapping plus proven/unknown relationship state; unknown cleanup rejects reliable registration. |
| functional_groups PK(group_id, revision) | revision>=1; stable content hash; description/labels, source provenance and override lineage; schema/estimator version; descriptor byte cost>=0. Unassigned entries are indexed independently, never dropped. |
| group_memberships PK(group_id, revision, binding_id) | FK group revision RESTRICT; canonical binding and selected contract revision; lifecycle_unit_id; full type/dependency closure through versioned group_dependencies and type pins. Reject unresolved/cyclic/oversize activation instead of selecting part of a lifecycle unit. |
| group_activations PK(client_id, credential_epoch, mcp_session_id, activation_revision) | FK clients RESTRICT; revision>=1; owner/session/credential scope, catalog/device/policy epochs, complete desired group-hash set, byte/token-estimate limits and immutable view ETag. Active pointer update CAS; no cross-client cursor/view reuse. |
| subscriptions PK(subscription_id) | client_id FK clients RESTRICT; credential_epoch; stream_epoch; revision>=1; state CHECK registering/active/cancelling/closed/orphaned/outcome_unknown; registration/cleanup/schema/type/wire pins; consumed registration-consent record; lease_expires_at and approved_max_expiry; event/byte/inline/read quotas>0; accepted/durable/loss cursors; cleanup outcome. Index(client_id,state,lease_expires_at). |
| subscription_events PK(subscription_id, stream_epoch, sequence) | FK subscription RESTRICT; positive64-bit sequence; method/schema/correlation/time; typed payload/resource/error discriminator; payload byte count>=0; immutable event digest. Index(subscription_id,stream_epoch,sequence). Sequence is encoded as a decimal string in JSON. |
| subscription_cleanup PK(subscription_id, cleanup_revision) | FK subscription; exact recorded cleanup capability/token mapping; state and last outcome, no arbitrary operation under revoked client authority. |

callback.status input is {subscription_id?:string, renew?:boolean=false, expected_revision?:positive_integer, requested_lease_seconds?:integer}. Without an ID it lists only the authenticated client's subscriptions with bounded cursor/limit and renew=false. A single result returns state, revision, lease expiry, quota/usage, oldest/newest retained cursors, cleanup status and sanitized reason. renew=true requires an owned active subscription, expected_revision CAS and a requested duration1..300seconds, capped by the client's max_lease, original registration's approved maximum expiry and current credential/contract validity. It cannot revive closed/orphaned/unknown registrations. Missing or stale revision conflicts without renewal; extending beyond original consent requires a new explicit admission/Ask decision. callback.status is not advertised universally read-only because renew mutates lease state. callback.cancel input {subscription_id, expected_revision?, cancellation_id} is owned and idempotent, suppresses future delivery and returns delivery_closed, cleanup_confirmed/cleanup_unknown and new revision; retries never repeat an uncertain side effect blindly.

Ask is consumed once for the exact registration, event/read scope and bounded lease, not for an unlimited conversation. Event publication/reads require the current rule to remain Ask or Allow, the consumed registration-consent record/pins/lease to remain valid and the client epoch to match. No new prompt is needed per event/read. Deny, revoked credential or expired consent immediately suppresses payload and queues recorded cleanup. A renewal cannot expand that original approved scope. Pending ordinary approvals still die on restart; persisted registration consent is a consumed bounded record, not a reusable owner approval for new calls. Restart marks OS registrations orphaned while retained journal access remains subject to the still-valid explicit read consent and current policy.

Binder callback threads copy bounded typed data into a bounded broker accept queue and return promptly; synchronous local reply selection never waits for SQLite. The accept queue counts against both per-subscription and client quotas, with proposed queue cap128events/256KiB within the existing total quotas. A single worker assigns commit order and batches at most64events or16ms of queue age per transaction using synchronous=FULL, preserving security-relevant storage policy. An event becomes replayable only after commit; precommit crash loss is disclosed, and durable cursors never acknowledge uncommitted events. Async queue order is acceptance order across threads, not physical source causality. Overflow and disk failure follow the explicit loss/terminal policy; no writer wait leaks back onto Binder threads. No global SQLite durability downgrade is introduced.

The base no-query events_uri returns a bounded status document with state, oldest/newest retained cursors and the exact next-read URI template; it does not repeatedly return an old first page. Expanded cursor URIs page history. Transport SSE replay defaults to nondurable; this does not remove the separately retained application journal. Every initialized StreamableHTTP session is assigned Mcp-Session-Id, and post-initialize requests without it receive400; ordinary GET/POST/DELETE/reconnect authenticate and enforce that scope. Session expiry requires reinitialization; same-principal journal reattachment is explicit and never transfers a live callback to another principal.

## Pairing, owner authentication and boot lifecycle

Pairing uses a separate POST /pair route on the same listener, outside MCP tools and open only during a local owner's bounded pairing window. It requires the paired_tls profile: the agent pins the server fingerprint supplied through the owner-visible local QR/channel before sending any proof; reduced_http rejects pairing. A one-use server nonce plus agent nonce, fingerprint and exact proposed label hash are length-prefixed into HMAC-SHA256 under the random bootstrap secret with domain pair-v1. The secret itself is never sent. Nonces expire after60seconds, are single-use and rate-limited; the response returns the new bearer once over the pinned TLS channel. No secret/proof/credential goes to logs. Approval binds the proposed principal and fingerprint; an expired/conflicting window fails without creating an active client. This is an explicit protocol design, not an implemented cryptographic handshake claim.

Fresh local owner authentication uses Android BiometricPrompt with BIOMETRIC_STRONG or DEVICE_CREDENTIAL on the reference API35+ profile. Pairing, credential reveal/rotation, grants, admin enablement and sensitive export require a successful prompt within30seconds, bound to a single nonce, exact command, revision/target scope and current foreground activity. Ask policy may require the same mechanism by profile, with timeout/missing UI denying. If device authentication is unavailable, sensitive owner transitions remain blocked; the bridge does not weaken the profile or change device security settings. Every remote input/capture path is quiesced before protected surfaces appear and remains excluded until completion/dismissal.

The unexported boot receiver is directBootAware and handles LOCKED_BOOT_COMPLETED only to read the minimal nonsecret device-protected mirror and record Waiting for unlock; it performs no network, catalog migration or credential-encrypted access there. BOOT_COMPLETED/ACTION_USER_UNLOCKED reconciles the mirror against credential-encrypted desired state and requests an eligible foreground-service start. An inconsistent mirror defaults off. The selected candidate foreground-service type is specialUse with a truthful declared subtype describing the local authorized MCP binding gateway; eligibility, platform prerequisites and distribution review are release gates, not presumed approval. No connectedDevice/dataSync label is used unless the actual product behavior qualifies; unsupported start remains explicit.

Wireless-debugging endpoint discovery uses supported NsdManager/mDNS service discovery for _adb-tls-pairing._tcp and _adb-tls-connect._tcp when visible in the authorized network environment, with owner-entered host:port fallback. Same-device discovery can fail on an OEM/network and must remain an explicit state. Discovered names/ports are untrusted until TLS peer identity matches the paired ADB profile. Never scan arbitrary ports or trust a stale endpoint after boot/toggle/network change.

screen.state caps inline PNG bytes at2MiB before base64 and total JSON result at3MiB; oversized capture is explicitly downscaled with the image-to-display transform or returned as a scoped bounded resource, never silently cropped. Fixed reviewed display-selecting screencap/input variants route a captured display consistently; unsupported per-display routing is explicit. input.tap validates display ID, rotation, capture/transform freshness and bounds against the admitted target. No automatic recapture/tap or replay of an uncertain gesture is implied.

## Named Kotlin HTTP evaluation target

The evaluation candidate is official MCP Kotlin SDK0.15.0, commit76b8e7d63aac39fe41e03684e3b91a6c3724a8da, with the Ktor CIO server engine as the pure-JVM hosting candidate. The official source documents StreamableHTTP/CIO integration; that is not Android or exact2025-11-25 conformance proof. Pin the candidate's compatible Ktor/Kotlin dependency graph during the implementation spike; test initialization/session/header/SSE/resource behavior against the pinned wire baseline and reject/patch incompatible behavior rather than upgrade protocol silently. Paired HTTPS needs a verified TLS-capable Android engine/tunnel adapter; CIO support alone is not evidence of the required server TLS profile. Until that gate passes, do not ship a sensitive cleartext fallback.

LoopbackHost is the Kotlin interface owning bind/start/stop/readiness, exact listener identity and TLS configuration, Host/Origin checks, credential extraction,256KiB request/nesting limits, authenticated session routing and bounded SSE queues. McpServer owns protocol semantics above it. No engine decides authorization. The local package's Gradle build generates documentation only, not this application or SDK integration. Sources: [official SDK candidate](https://github.com/modelcontextprotocol/kotlin-sdk/tree/76b8e7d63aac39fe41e03684e3b91a6c3724a8da), [Ktor server engines](https://ktor.io/docs/server-engines.html).

BEGIN FULL ARTIFACT {"file": "design-c4/build.gradle.kts", "size_bytes": 15812, "sha256": "f2a507e3e9db5251339d23ef72846504b54211fe0950a6b2cca53a81e17e8f3c"}
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.util.Base64

plugins { base }

val toolDir = layout.projectDirectory.dir(".tools")
val diagrams = layout.projectDirectory.dir("docs/diagrams")
val architecture = layout.projectDirectory.dir("architecture")
val pumlJar = toolDir.file("plantuml-1.2025.10.jar")
val cli = providers.environmentVariable("STRUCTURIZR_CLI").orElse("/usr/local/structurizr-cli/structurizr.sh")
val expectedNativeViews = listOf("Context", "Containers", "ServerComponents", "ServerCoreComponents", "ExecutionComponents", "ArcComponents", "Startup", "InvocationFlow", "CallbackFlow", "GroupFlow", "ArcSession", "OwnerTransition", "Deployment")
val expectedCodeViews = listOf("server-core-classes", "server-execution-classes", "arc-ui-classes", "arc-controller-classes", "arc-target-classes")
val expectedUiViews = (1..11).map { "SCR%02d".format(it) } + (1..6).map { "STB%02d".format(it) }

fun sha256(file: File): String {
    val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
    file.inputStream().use { input ->
        val buffer = ByteArray(65536)
        while (true) { val count = input.read(buffer); if (count < 0) break; digest.update(buffer, 0, count) }
    }
    return digest.digest().joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
}
fun javaCommand(): String = File(System.getProperty("java.home"), "bin/java").absolutePath
fun copyVectorWithCanvas(source: File, target: File) {
    val svg = source.readText().replace(Regex("<!--SRC=\\[.*?\\]-->", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL), "")
    val end = svg.indexOf('>', svg.indexOf("<svg")) + 1
    check(end > 0) { "Invalid generated SVG root: $source" }
    target.writeText(svg.substring(0, end) + "<rect data-canvas=\"white\" x=\"0\" y=\"0\" width=\"100%\" height=\"100%\" fill=\"#ffffff\"/>" + svg.substring(end))
}

val verifyToolchain by tasks.registering {
    inputs.files("toolchain-lock.json", "renderer-lock.json", "tooling/marp-package-lock.json", pumlJar)
    doLast {
        check(pumlJar.asFile.isFile) { "Pinned PlantUML missing: bootstrap task-local dependencies first" }
        val lock = file("toolchain-lock.json").readText()
        check(lock.contains(sha256(pumlJar.asFile))) { "PlantUML checksum differs from recorded toolchain" }
        val pins = Regex("\"file\":\\s*\"([^\"]+)\".*?\"sha256\":\\s*\"([a-f0-9]{64})\"", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL).findAll(lock).toList()
        check(pins.size == 5) { "Build-tool checksum manifest incomplete" }
        pins.forEach { pin -> check(sha256(file(pin.groupValues[1])) == pin.groupValues[2]) { "Build-tool checksum mismatch: ${pin.groupValues[1]}" } }
        check(file(cli.get()).isFile) { "Use the documented digest-pinned local Structurizr container" }
        val renderer = file("renderer-lock.json").readText()
        fun pin(name: String): String = Regex("\"$name\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]+)\"").find(renderer)?.groupValues?.get(1) ?: error("Missing renderer pin: $name")
        check(System.getProperty("java.version") == pin("java_version")) { "JDK differs from pinned renderer profile; use documented existing image" }
        val rendererPins = Regex("\"path\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]+)\".*?\"sha256\"\\s*:\\s*\"([a-f0-9]{64})\"", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL).findAll(renderer).toList()
        check(rendererPins.size == 5) { "Renderer identity lock incomplete" }
        rendererPins.forEach { value ->
            val selected = file(cli.get()).parentFile.resolve(value.groupValues[1])
            check(selected.isFile && sha256(selected) == value.groupValues[2]) { "Structurizr renderer identity mismatch: $selected" }
        }
        check(sha256(file("tooling/marp-package-lock.json")) == pin("marp_lock_sha256")) { "Committed Marp lock differs from pin" }
        val cachedLock = file(".tools/marp/package-lock.json")
        val cachedPackage = file(".tools/marp/node_modules/@marp-team/marp-cli/package.json")
        check(cachedLock.isFile && cachedPackage.isFile) { "Offline Marp cache missing; run documented task-local bootstrap before offline build" }
        check(sha256(cachedLock) == pin("marp_lock_sha256")) { "Cached Marp lock mismatch; bootstrap exact committed lock" }
        check(Regex("\"version\"\\s*:\\s*\"${Regex.escape(pin("marp_version"))}\"").containsMatchIn(cachedPackage.readText())) { "Cached Marp CLI version mismatch" }
        val node = providers.environmentVariable("NODE_BIN").orElse("node").get()
        val version = ProcessBuilder(node, "--version").redirectErrorStream(true).start()
        val versionText = version.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText().trim()
        check(version.waitFor() == 0 && versionText == pin("node_version")) { "Node runtime mismatch/missing: expected ${pin("node_version")}, received $versionText" }
    }
}

val extractCodeViews by tasks.registering {
    inputs.file("architecture/code-diagrams.md")
    outputs.dir(layout.buildDirectory.dir("code-views"))
    doLast {
        val source = file("architecture/code-diagrams.md").readText()
        val blocks = Regex("## ([a-z-]+)[^\\n]*\\n.*?```plantuml\\n(.*?)\\n```", RegexOption.DOT_MATCHES_ALL).findAll(source).associate { it.groupValues[1] to it.groupValues[2] }
        check(blocks.keys == expectedCodeViews.toSet()) { "Missing/unexpected editable code view: ${blocks.keys}" }
        val out = layout.buildDirectory.dir("code-views").get().asFile.apply { mkdirs() }
        blocks.forEach { (key, body) -> File(out, "$key.puml").writeText(body + "\n") }
    }
}

val renderCodeViews by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(verifyToolchain, extractCodeViews)
    inputs.files(fileTree("build/code-views") { include("*.puml") }, pumlJar, "build.gradle.kts", "renderer-lock.json")
    outputs.files(expectedCodeViews.map { diagrams.file("$it.svg") })
    doLast {
        diagrams.asFile.mkdirs()
        val sources = fileTree("build/code-views") { include("*.puml") }.files.sorted()
        val process = ProcessBuilder(listOf(javaCommand(), "-Djava.awt.headless=true", "-jar", pumlJar.asFile.absolutePath, "-tsvg", "-nometadata", "-charset", "UTF-8") + sources.map { it.absolutePath }).inheritIO().start()
        check(process.waitFor() == 0) { "Code-view rendering failed" }
        expectedCodeViews.forEach { copyVectorWithCanvas(file("build/code-views/$it.svg"), diagrams.file("$it.svg").asFile) }
    }
}

val validateArchitecture by tasks.registering(Exec::class) {
    dependsOn(renderCodeViews)
    inputs.files("architecture/workspace.dsl", "architecture/code-diagrams.md", "toolchain-lock.json")
    inputs.files(expectedCodeViews.map { diagrams.file("$it.svg") })
    commandLine(cli.get(), "validate", "-workspace", architecture.file("workspace.dsl").asFile.absolutePath)
    doLast {
        layout.buildDirectory.file("receipts/architecture-validation.txt").get().asFile.apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeText("Structurizr validation exited0\nworkspace_sha256=${sha256(file("architecture/workspace.dsl"))}\n") }
    }
}

val exportArchitecture by tasks.registering(Exec::class) {
    dependsOn(validateArchitecture)
    inputs.files("architecture/workspace.dsl", "architecture/render-skin.puml", "build.gradle.kts")
    outputs.dir(layout.buildDirectory.dir("structurizr-export"))
    commandLine(cli.get(), "export", "-workspace", architecture.file("workspace.dsl").asFile.absolutePath, "-format", "plantuml", "-output", layout.buildDirectory.dir("structurizr-export").get().asFile.absolutePath)
    doLast {
        // Pinned exporter does not emit the global font property. Apply only this
        // local rendering profile after model export; never rewrite model semantics.
        val include = "!include ../../architecture/render-skin.puml"
        fileTree("build/structurizr-export") { include("*.puml") }.files.forEach { source ->
            val body = source.readText()
            if (!body.contains(include)) source.writeText(body.replaceFirst("@startuml", "@startuml\n$include"))
        }
    }
}

val generateArchitectureDiagrams by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(exportArchitecture)
    inputs.files(fileTree("build/structurizr-export") { include("*.puml") }, pumlJar, "build.gradle.kts", "renderer-lock.json", "architecture/render-skin.puml")
    outputs.files(expectedNativeViews.map { diagrams.file("structurizr-$it.svg") })
    doLast {
        val sources = fileTree("build/structurizr-export") { include("*.puml") }.files.sorted()
        check(sources.size >= expectedNativeViews.size) { "Structurizr did not export all native C4 views" }
        val process = ProcessBuilder(listOf(javaCommand(), "-Djava.awt.headless=true", "-jar", pumlJar.asFile.absolutePath, "-tsvg", "-nometadata", "-charset", "UTF-8") + sources.map { it.absolutePath }).inheritIO().start()
        check(process.waitFor() == 0) { "C4 export rendering failed" }
        expectedNativeViews.forEach { key ->
            val source = file("build/structurizr-export/structurizr-$key.svg")
            check(source.isFile && source.length() > 100) { "Missing generated C4 SVG: $key" }
            copyVectorWithCanvas(source, diagrams.file(source.name).asFile)
        }
    }
}

val generateUiAssets by tasks.registering(Copy::class) {
    inputs.file("docs/ui-asset-manifest.json")
    from("ui-svg") { include("*.svg") }
    into("docs/screens")
    doFirst {
        val manifestIds = Regex("\"id\"\\s*:\\s*\"((?:SCR|STB)[0-9]+)\"").findAll(file("docs/ui-asset-manifest.json").readText()).map { it.groupValues[1] }.toList()
        check(manifestIds.size == manifestIds.toSet().size && manifestIds.toSet() == expectedUiViews.toSet()) { "UI manifest does not declare the exact required screens/storyboards" }
        check(fileTree("ui-svg") { include("*.svg") }.files.map { it.nameWithoutExtension }.toSet() == expectedUiViews.toSet()) { "Canonical UI source asset missing or unexpected" }
    }
    doLast {
        val exported = fileTree("docs/screens") { include("*.svg") }.files
        check(exported.map { it.nameWithoutExtension }.toSet() == expectedUiViews.toSet()) { "Exported UI assets differ from manifest" }
        exported.forEach { check(sha256(it) == sha256(file("ui-svg/${it.name}"))) { "Edit canonical ui-svg source, not generated copy: $it" } }
    }
}

val renderStoryboards by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(generateUiAssets)
    inputs.files("storyboards/storyboard.md", ".tools/marp/package-lock.json", fileTree("docs/screens"))
    outputs.file("docs/storyboards.html")
    doLast {
        var source = file("storyboards/storyboard.md").readText()
        source = Regex("!\\[([^]]*)]\\(([^)]+\\.svg)\\)").replace(source) { match ->
            val image = file("storyboards").resolve(match.groupValues[2]).canonicalFile
            check(image.toPath().startsWith(projectDir.canonicalFile.toPath())) { "Storyboard image outside package" }
            "![${match.groupValues[1]}](data:image/svg+xml;base64,${Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(image.readBytes())})"
        }
        val inline = layout.buildDirectory.file("storyboards.inline.md").get().asFile.apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeText(source) }
        val node = providers.environmentVariable("NODE_BIN").orElse("node").get()
        val process = ProcessBuilder(node, file(".tools/marp/node_modules/@marp-team/marp-cli/marp-cli.js").absolutePath, inline.absolutePath, "--html", "-o", file("docs/storyboards.html").absolutePath).inheritIO().start()
        val exit = process.waitFor()
        check(exit == 0) { "Pinned Marp slideshow rendering failed (exit=$exit)" }
        check(!Regex("(?:src|href)=[\"'](?:https?:)?//|url\\(\\s*[\"']?(?:https?:)?//", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE).containsMatchIn(file("docs/storyboards.html").readText())) { "Slideshow contains external assets" }
    }
}

val verifyDesignDocs by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(generateArchitectureDiagrams, generateUiAssets, renderStoryboards)
    inputs.files(fileTree("docs"), "README.md", "renderer-lock.json", "architecture/workspace.dsl", "architecture/code-diagrams.md", "storyboards/storyboard.md")
    outputs.file(layout.buildDirectory.file("receipts/docs-validation.txt"))
    doLast {
        val svgFiles = fileTree("docs") { include("**/*.svg") }.files
        val expectedSvgPaths = expectedNativeViews.map { "diagrams/structurizr-$it.svg" } + expectedCodeViews.map { "diagrams/$it.svg" } + expectedUiViews.map { "screens/$it.svg" }
        check(svgFiles.map { it.relativeTo(file("docs")).invariantSeparatorsPath }.toSet() == expectedSvgPaths.toSet()) { "Missing or unexpected vector diagram/screen assets" }
        svgFiles.forEach { svg ->
            val text = svg.readText()
            check(text.contains("<svg") && text.contains("<text")) { "SVG must contain vector text: $svg" }
            check(!text.contains("<!--SRC=[")) { "Stale embedded renderer metadata: $svg" }
            check(!text.contains("<image", ignoreCase = true) && !text.contains("<script", ignoreCase = true)) { "Raster/script not allowed in vector SVG: $svg" }
        }
        (fileTree("docs") { include("**/*.md") }.files + setOf(file("README.md"), file("storyboards/storyboard.md"), file("architecture/code-diagrams.md"))).forEach { md ->
            val text = md.readText()
            check(!text.contains("```mer" + "maid", ignoreCase = true)) { "Obsolete diagram source in current docs" }
            Regex("!?\\[[^]]*]\\(([^)]+)\\)").findAll(text).forEach { match ->
                val target = match.groupValues[1].substringBefore('#').trim('<', '>')
                if (target.isNotEmpty() && !target.contains("://") && !target.startsWith("data:")) check(md.parentFile.resolve(target).isFile) { "Broken relative link: $target in $md" }
            }
        }
        layout.buildDirectory.file("receipts/docs-validation.txt").get().asFile.apply { parentFile.mkdirs(); writeText("Vector SVGs=${svgFiles.size}; Markdown relative links valid; no raster substitutions; no public rendering\n") }
    }
}

val generateArtifactManifest by tasks.registering {
    dependsOn(verifyDesignDocs)
    inputs.files(fileTree("docs") { exclude("checksums.sha256") }, fileTree("architecture"), fileTree("ui-svg"), fileTree("storyboards"), fileTree("gradle"), fileTree("tooling"), "README.md", "build.gradle.kts", "settings.gradle.kts", "gradle.properties", "gradlew", "gradlew.bat", "toolchain-lock.json", "renderer-lock.json")
    outputs.file("docs/checksums.sha256")
    doLast {
        val files = (fileTree("docs") { exclude("checksums.sha256") }.files + fileTree("architecture").files + fileTree("ui-svg").files + fileTree("storyboards").files + fileTree("gradle").files + fileTree("tooling").files + listOf("README.md", "build.gradle.kts", "settings.gradle.kts", "gradle.properties", "gradlew", "gradlew.bat", "toolchain-lock.json", "renderer-lock.json").map { file(it) }).filter { !it.path.contains("__pycache__") }.sortedBy { it.relativeTo(projectDir).invariantSeparatorsPath }
        file("docs/checksums.sha256").writeText(files.joinToString("\n", postfix="\n") { "${sha256(it)}  ${it.relativeTo(projectDir).invariantSeparatorsPath}" })
    }
}

tasks.register<Zip>("packageDesignDocs") {
    dependsOn(generateArtifactManifest)
    archiveFileName.set("Android MCP Bridge Design Portable.zip")
    destinationDirectory.set(layout.buildDirectory.dir("distributions"))
    isPreserveFileTimestamps = false
    isReproducibleFileOrder = true
    from("docs") { into("docs") }
    from("architecture") { into("architecture") }
    from("ui-svg") { into("ui-svg") }
    from("storyboards") { into("storyboards") }
    from("gradle") { into("gradle") }
    from("tooling") { into("tooling"); exclude("**/__pycache__/**") }
    from("build/receipts") { into("validation") }
    from(listOf("README.md", "build.gradle.kts", "settings.gradle.kts", "gradle.properties", "gradlew", "gradlew.bat", "toolchain-lock.json", "renderer-lock.json"))
}
